Method: 基于WordNet超类图中词语距离与共现频率之间的经验关系，建立词向量（如word2vec）Gram矩阵的谱分析理论模型，并在满足一定正性和衰减条件下，推导其特征向量如何反映从粗到细的分类层次结构，随后在实际embedding和Gemma 2B unembedding中验证该理论预测。

Result: 理论证明并实验证实，词嵌入的前导特征向量呈现出从广泛分类分支到更细子分支的层次分裂几何结构，这种结构与WordNet树形图一致；该现象不仅存在于word2vec中，也显著存在于Gemma 2B的unembedding中。

Conclusion: 语言模型中概念的层次几何结构未必源于专门的层级处理机制，而是可以从词语共现统计的谱结构中自然涌现，为理解大模型中语义结构的形成提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We propose a distributional theory of how hypernymy -- the ``is-a'' relation between general and specific concepts -- is encoded geometrically in language representations. Starting from the empirically verified assumption that words closer on the WordNet hypernym graph co-occur more often, we characterize theoretically the spectrum of the resulting embedding Gram matrix of word2vec embeddings. Under mild positivity and decay conditions on the co-occurrence kernel, we prove that the leading eigenvectors first separate broad taxonomic branches and then progressively finer sub-branches, producing a \emph{hierarchical splitting geometry} with a coarse-to-fine spectral organization that mirrors the tree. We confirm these predictions in word2vec embeddings across many sampled WordNet subtrees, and show that the same signature extends strikingly well to Gemma 2B unembeddings. Our results indicate that hierarchical concept geometry in LLMs need not reflect a hierarchy-specific functional mechanism, but emerges from the spectral structure of pairwise word statistics.

</details>


### [116] [Multilingual Knowledge Transfer under Data Constraints via Lexical Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23885)
*Anastasiia Sedova,Natalie Schluter,Skyler Seto,Maartje ter Hoeve*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为LINK的数据级干预方法，通过在高资源语言预训练数据中进行词汇替换，以零成本的双语词汇表实现跨语言知识迁移，显著提升低资源语言下的下游任务性能，并加快训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于许多语言缺乏足够的平行数据、翻译系统或额外模型，现有的跨语言知识迁移方法受限，因此需要一种低成本且高效的知识迁移方法。

Method: 在高资源语言（如英语）的预训练数据中，按一定比例随机使用双语词汇表将部分单词替换为其目标语言的翻译，从而在预训练阶段增强跨语言知识迁移，无需额外训练或复杂辅助工具。

Result: 在八种语言和五种模型规模上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了目标语言下游任务的表现，并实现了最高达2倍的训练加速。

Conclusion: LINK是一种简单、低成本且高效的方法，能够在没有平行语料或翻译系统的情况下有效促进跨语言知识迁移，适用于广泛的语言和技术场景。

Abstract: Cross-lingual knowledge transfer is critical for building high-performing multilingual language models for languages with insufficient training data. When target language data is scarce, the knowledge required for many downstream tasks involving scientific reasoning, commonsense inference, and world knowledge must be acquired primarily from the high-resource language, making effective knowledge transfer essential. Existing methods for improving such cross-lingual knowledge transfer require large amounts of parallel data, translation systems, auxiliary models, or additional training stages that are largely unavailable for many languages. We propose LINK - a data-level intervention method that improves knowledge transfer during model pretraining through lexical substitutions in high-resource part of pretraining data using bilingual vocabularies. For a given replacement ratio, randomly selected words in a portion of the high-resource (English) training corpus are swapped with their word-level translations, requiring no additional model training and only a bilingual vocabulary, which can be obtained at near-zero cost for virtually any language. Evaluation on eight languages across five model sizes shows notable improvements on downstream tasks in the target language, with up to a 2x speedup in training to reach equivalent performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [RMA: an Agentic System for Research-Level Mathematical Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22875)
*Zelin Zhao,Bo Yuan,Jaemoo Choi,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Research Math Agents (RMA)的智能体框架，用于自动求解研究级别的数学问题，在First Proof基准上优于GPT-5.2R和Aletheia等强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要集中于竞赛数学或形式化定理证明，难以应对需要长程推理、文献支撑和迭代优化的研究级数学问题。

Method: 将研究级数学问题求解分解为多个专用模块（如问题分析、文献检索、知识库构建、证明验证等），通过初始化器、提议者和验证者智能体在共享结构化记忆中协同工作，实现多角色、多轮次的迭代反馈与证明生成。

Result: 在由专家提供的10个研究级数学问题构成的First Proof基准上，RMA解决了其中8个，生成的证明更逻辑严密且可读性更强，并优于GPT-5.2R和Aletheia等基线模型。

Conclusion: RMA通过模块化设计、迭代 refinement 和验证器反馈的协同作用，有效提升了对研究级数学问题的自动推理能力，展示了AI在前沿数学研究中的潜力。

Abstract: We present $\textbf{Research Math Agents (RMA)}$, an agentic framework for automated reasoning on research-level mathematical problems. Unlike prior studies centered on competition mathematics or formal theorem proving, RMA targets research-level mathematical problems that require long-horizon reasoning, literature grounding, and iterative proof refinement. RMA decomposes research-level proof solving into specialized modules for problem analysis, literature search and understanding, fair comparison, knowledge-bank construction, and proof verification, all coordinated by initializer, proposer, and verifier agents through a shared structured memory. Within this unified framework, these agents operate in a multi-role, multi-round workflow, collaboratively generating, refining, and verifying candidate proofs through iterative feedback. We evaluate RMA on the First Proof benchmark, which consists of ten research-level problems contributed by expert mathematicians across diverse domains. Through comprehensive expert evaluation, RMA outperforms strong baselines on the First Proof benchmark, including GPT-5.2R and Aletheia, solving eight out of ten research problems and producing more logically sound and readable proofs. Our comprehensive ablation studies further show that performance gains arise from the interaction of structured reasoning modules, iterative refinement, and verifier-based feedback, rather than any single component. Our solutions and implementations will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [118] [ImProver 2: Iteratively Self-Improving LMs for Neurosymbolic Proof Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22885)
*Riyaz Ahuja,Tate Rowney,Jeremy Avigad,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ImProver 2是一个用于Lean 4中自动证明优化的神经符号框架，通过数据高效的专家迭代流程和暴露形式化结构与轻量级非形式化抽象的支架，实现了在小模型上优化复杂证明的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着形式化数学库的迅速扩展，需要对已验证的证明进行重构以提高可维护性，并改善神经定理证明器的训练数据质量，但现有方法面临目标异构、数据稀缺和计算成本高等挑战。

Method: 提出ImProver 2框架，结合数据高效的专业迭代流程和揭示形式结构与轻量非正式抽象的神经符号支架，并引入一套衡量证明结构特性的指标。

Result: 训练了一个70亿参数的模型，在相同模型家族中优于更大规模的模型，并在多个指标上与中等前沿模型相当；神经符号支架显著提升了大小模型的性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过适当的支架设计和训练，小型模型也能有效重构复杂多样的研究级证明，使证明优化成为可扩展且可学习的任务。

Abstract: Formal mathematics libraries are rapidly expanding, creating a growing need to refactor verified proofs for maintainability and to improve training data quality for neural provers. However, scalable proof optimization is hindered by heterogeneous and heuristically specified objectives, scarce data, and high training and inference costs. To overcome these challenges, we introduce ImProver 2, a neurosymbolic framework for automated proof optimization in Lean 4. ImProver 2 combines a data-efficient expert-iteration pipeline with a scaffold that exposes formal structure alongside lightweight informal abstractions. We further introduce a suite of metrics capturing structural proof properties. Using ImProver 2, we train a 7B-parameter model that outperforms orders-of-magnitude larger models within the same model family, and is competitive with mid-tier frontier models across metrics. We additionally demonstrate that our neurosymbolic scaffold significantly improves performance across both small and frontier models. We show that with proper scaffolding and training, small models can effectively restructure research-level proofs over complex and varied metrics, matching substantially larger systems and establishing proof optimization as a scalable, learnable task.

</details>


### [119] [Mediative Fuzzy Logic: From Type-1 Foundations to Type-2, Type-3 and Quantum Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22900)
*Oscar Montiel Ross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的Mediative Fuzzy Logic框架，涵盖从type-1到量子扩展的多层级形式，通过犹豫与矛盾控制的凸聚合机制建模真值，并在自动驾驶制动系统的传感器融合示例中验证了其在不完整和矛盾信息下实现安全优先决策的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mediative Fuzzy Logic虽被用于处理模糊控制与决策中的冲突评估，但其逻辑与语义基础在超越type-1的情形下仍不完善，亟需统一且可扩展的理论框架。

Method: 将mediative算子建模为受犹豫与矛盾调控的凸聚合，采用连续双格结构表示独立的真假值对，构建基于t-范数模糊逻辑并含mediative连接词的命题系统，并发展至区间type-2、粒型type-3及量子（希尔伯特空间上的密度算子）语义扩展。

Result: 建立了系统的可靠性、亚协调性以及对无中介公式的保守性，实现了多层级语义的一致性；高阶形式在适当条件下可退化为type-1情形，确保跨层级的逻辑连贯性。

Conclusion: 该框架为异构、不完整和轻度矛盾信息下的智能决策提供了透明、保守且安全优先的逻辑基础，支持未来多层次智能系统的发展。

Abstract: Mediative Fuzzy Logic was conceived as a practical scheme for reconciling hesitant or conflicting assessments in fuzzy control and decision-making. However, its logical and semantic foundations remain underdeveloped, especially beyond operational type-1 settings. This article develops a unified account of the type-1 core together with interval type-2, granular type-3, and quantum extensions. We characterize the mediative operator as a convex aggregation controlled by hesitation and contradiction, model mediative truth values as independent truth-falsity pairs in a continuous bilattice-like structure, and introduce a propositional system extending a standard t-norm-based fuzzy logic with a mediative connective. We establish soundness, paraconsistency, and conservativity over the underlying fuzzy base for formulas without mediation, and formulate coherent semantic extensions to interval type-2 truth values, granule-indexed local evaluations, and effects and density operators on Hilbert spaces. An autonomous-braking sensor-fusion example illustrates how the framework supports transparent, conservative, and safety-first decisions under incomplete, heterogeneous, and mildly contradictory evidence. Under suitable assumptions, the higher-level formulations reduce to the type-1 case, clarifying coherence across levels and reliably supporting future work in intelligent decision systems.

</details>


### [120] [The Deterministic Horizon: Impossibility Results as Design Specifications for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23024)
*Dongxin Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将计算理论中的不可能性结果转化为AI系统设计规则，提出由模型架构决定的推理精度上限——确定性地平线，并揭示其在多个子领域的应用，形成包含十六个可计算边界的设计规范目录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型已广泛应用于软件编写、法律文书起草和临床记录生成，但计算的基本极限（如图灵、阿罗及无免费午餐定理）仍制约其能力。本文旨在将这些理论上的不可能性结果从学术好奇转变为实用的设计原则，以构建更可信的人工智能系统。

Method: 通过信息论与电路复杂度分析，推导出基于残差流容量不变量的确定性地平线，可在部署前根据层数和嵌入宽度计算；结合模块化指数运算的电路复杂度下界，并将该框架推广至偏好学习、检索系统、拍卖机制和零知识验证等多个领域，建立统一的不可能性-规范方法论。

Result: 发现模型架构本身设定了推理准确率的硬性上限，超过临界推理深度后任何训练手段均无法提升性能；测量了12种Transformer架构的地平线值介于19到31之间；微调最优长度轨迹可恢复不足4个百分点的性能；在多个子领域中识别出16个具体规范，其中包含可量化的违规成本与构造性设计规则，并证明了两个组合规则，一个诚实障碍，四个开放问题。

Conclusion: 每一个AI的基本极限都可以转化为一条设计规则，通过提前识别和量化这些边界，可以指导更可靠、高效的AI系统设计，为生成式AI研究提供新的方法论基础。

Abstract: Large language models now write software, draft legal documents, and produce clinical notes, yet fundamental limits, from Turing and Arrow to the No Free Lunch theorems, shape what computation can do. This thesis turns such impossibility results from curiosities into design rules. Its flagship result proves an accuracy ceiling set by architecture alone: past a critical reasoning depth, no amount of training moves it, at any adapter rank, sample size, or loss function. Computable before deployment from layer count and embedding width, this Deterministic Horizon is measured between nineteen and thirty-one across twelve transformer architectures, and fine-tuning on optimal-length traces recovers under four percentage points. The mechanism is a capacity invariant of the residual stream, and an information-theoretic conversion yields super-exponential accuracy decay past the horizon. An unconditional circuit-complexity lower bound for modular exponentiation against constant-depth prime-modulus circuits complements this result. The same argument recasts across subfields: preference learning under any misspecified model jumps discontinuously in sample complexity; multi-stage retrieval pipelines require at least as many independent metrics as stages; standard truthful auctions fail for agents with prompt-dependent valuations; and zero-knowledge verification of neural inference pays a measured overhead of one hundred ten to one hundred ninety times per non-linear activation. Together these form a catalogue of sixteen specifications, each pairing a computable boundary, a quantified violation cost, and a constructive design rule: two compositions are proved, one pairing is an honest obstruction, and four remain open. The impossibility-specification methodology is offered for the generative research programme that trustworthy AI may need. Every fundamental limit of AI is also a design rule.

</details>


### [121] [PathCal: State-Aware Reflection-Marker Calibration for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23074)
*Lingyu Jiang,Zirui Li,Shuo Xing,Peiran Li,Tsubasa Takahashi,Dengzhe Hou,Zhengzhong Tu,Kazunori Yamada,Fangzhou Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的解码控制器PathCal，通过区分不同类型的反思标记并在局部不确定状态时进行干预，来校准推理路径，从而在保持或提高准确率的同时减少生成长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的研究通常将推理过程中的反思标记视为单一粗粒度类别，而忽略了它们在功能角色和影响时机上的差异。本文旨在揭示不同类型反思标记的作用差异，并利用这些差异优化推理过程。

Method: 通过类型化抑制和固定前缀干预实验，分析不同反思标记对准确性和生成长度的影响；提出PathCal方法，根据反思标记分布估计当前推理路径与竞争分支之间的局部竞争关系，并在必要时软性调整标记logits。

Result: 在六个推理基准上的实验表明，PathCal能够在不依赖外部验证器或多步采样的情况下，实现更好的效率-性能权衡，提升或保持准确性的同时缩短生成长度。

Conclusion: 区分反思标记的类型并仅在局部不确定性状态下进行干预，是一种有效的推理路径校准策略，PathCal为大语言模型的高效推理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Reasoning Language Models (LRMs) has paved the way for tackling complex reasoning tasks through test-time scaling by generating long-form Chain-of-Thought (CoT) trajectories during inference. Meanwhile, these trajectories often contain explicit reflection markers such as ``wait'', ``but'', and ``alternatively'', signaling hesitation, revision, and the consideration of alternative explorations, respectively. Recent studies on test-time control leverage such markers as lightweight handles for steering reasoning, typically treating them as a single coarse-grained category rather than distinguishing their distinct functional roles. In this paper, we conduct type-wise suppression and fixed-prefix intervention, revealing that reflection markers differ not only in their functional roles but also in when they exert the greatest influence. Specifically, different marker classes affect accuracy and generation length in distinct ways, and marker choices are most consequential before the model settles into a stable reasoning trajectory. Motivated by these findings, we introduce PathCal, a novel training-free decoding controller that calibrates reasoning paths by distinguishing marker types and intervening only at locally uncertain states. At each decoding step, PathCal utilizes the distribution over reflection-markers to estimate local competition between maintaining the current reasoning trajectory and initiating a competing branch, and softly rebalances marker logits when competing-branch evidence becomes excessive. Experiments across six reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PathCal achieves a better efficiency--performance trade-off, improving or preserving accuracy while reducing generation length, without relying on external verifiers or additional sampling.

</details>


### [122] [AutoResearch AI: Towards AI-Powered Research Automation for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23204)
*Guiyao Tie,Jiawen Shi,Dingjie Song,Yixiao Huang,Ziji Sheng,Xueyang Zhou,Daizong Liu,Pan Zhou,Yongchao Chen,Ran Xu,Lifang He,Qingsong Wen,Manling Li,Cong Lu,Shuai Li,Pengtao Xie,Yixuan Yuan,Rui Meng,Lei Xing,Lichao Sun,Caiming Xiong,Philip S. Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“AutoResearch”概念，系统梳理了AI驱动科研工作流自动化的现状与挑战，划分了从人类主导到AI主导的研究范式光谱，提出了五个关键评估维度，并指出自动化程度受领域条件制约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI在科学研究中的应用分散且缺乏统一框架，难以实现端到端的科研自动化，亟需系统性梳理与标准化评估体系以推动该领域发展。

Method: 通过提出“AutoResearch”这一概念框架，将现有研究按控制权分布划分为Vibe Research与AI-led系统，并围绕五个核心工作流环节进行系统分析，综合整理了AI科学家系统、协同研究框架、基准测试与开源基础设施。

Result: 建立了科研自动化领域的分类体系与组织框架，识别出证据保留、可重复性、弱方向拒绝等关键挑战，提出了新颖性、有效性、影响力、可靠性与溯源性五维评估标准。

Conclusion: 科研自动化是一个连续谱系，其自主性高度依赖于具体科学领域的结构化程度与验证效率，在高伦理责任或实体参与的场景中仍需人类深度参与。

Abstract: Scientific research is being reshaped by AI systems that move beyond isolated assistance toward longer-horizon workflows spanning literature grounding, hypothesis generation, experimentation, validation, reporting, and revision. This shift marks a transition from task-level AI for science to workflow-level research automation. Yet current systems remain fragmented, differing in autonomy, domain scope, execution environment, validation mechanism, and human oversight, while still struggling with evidence preservation, reproducibility, weak-direction rejection, provenance tracking, cross-domain robustness, and accountable scientific closure. This survey examines these developments through AutoResearch, defined as the developmental spectrum of AI-powered scientific workflow automation. Within it, Vibe Research denotes the human-steered region of prompt-based assistance and human-verified execution, whereas emerging AI-led systems coordinate larger portions of the discovery loop without achieving robust autonomy. We analyze how research systems redistribute control, evidence, execution, validation, and accountability across workflows and organize the field around five workflow conditions: literature and research grounding; hypothesis formation and planning; experimentation and tool use; feedback, validation, and review; and reporting and knowledge communication. We further synthesize AI scientist systems, mixed-initiative co-research frameworks, benchmarks, domain deployments, and open-source infrastructures. Finally, we propose five evaluation dimensions--novelty, validity, impact, reliability, and provenance--and show that AutoResearch autonomy is domain-conditioned, being more credible in structured, executable, and rapidly verifiable settings but limited in embodied, delayed, heterogeneous, ethical, or institutionally accountable contexts.

</details>


### [123] [Foundation Protocol: A Coordination Layer for Agentic Society](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23218)
*Bang Liu,Yongfeng Gu,Jiayi Zhang,Zhaoyang Yu,Sirui Hong,Maojia Song,Xiaoqiang Wang,Mingyi Deng,Zijie Zhuang,Ronghao Wang,Mingzhe Cao,Yutong Zhu,Xingjian Li,Yifan Wu,Jianhao Ruan,Yiran Peng,Shuangrui Chen,Jinlin Wang,Yizhang Lin,Dongjie Zhang,Dekun Wu,Chen Ma,Lizi Liao,Han Yu,Jian Pei,Heng Ji,Qiang Yang,Yuyu Luo,Chenglin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为Foundation Protocol（FP）的图优先协调层，旨在为人类与AI社会的协同提供基础设施，支持多实体协作、经济原语和可审计性，实现可组合性与不可协商的责任共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体在社会基础设施中扮演越来越重要的角色，系统扩展带来的协调瓶颈日益突出，亟需一种支持多方协作、价值交换与问责机制的统一协调框架。

Method: 设计并实现了一个图优先的协调协议FP，统一异构实体（如智能体、工具、人类、机构等），支持基于事件的协作、原生多方组织结构，并集成计量、收据、结算等经济原语，将策略、溯源和审计作为核心设计要素。

Result: FP能够封装并桥接现有协议而非取代它们，支持渐进式采用，降低集成与治理开销，实现了可组合的自主性与强问责性的平衡。

Conclusion: FP为人类-AI社会提供了一种开放、多元且可治理的共享协调基础设施，使协调本身成为可信赖的社会技术基础层。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are moving from tools into a layer of social infrastructure: they browse, purchase, deploy software, manage systems, and increasingly interact with one another. As these systems scale, the bottleneck shifts away from raw model capability toward coordination. Agents need to form reliable relationships, organize multi-agent work, exchange value, support an AI economy, and stay safe and accountable under real-world oversight. This paper introduces the Foundation Protocol (FP), a graph-first coordination layer for an emerging human-AI society. FP unifies heterogeneous entities, including agents, tools, resources, humans, institutions, and organizations, and supports native multi-party organization and event-based collaboration. It also provides economic primitives for metering, receipts, and settlement, and treats policy, provenance, and audit as first-class concerns. FP is designed to wrap and bridge existing protocols rather than replace them, enabling incremental adoption while reducing integration and governance overhead. The aim is to keep autonomous agency composable while keeping accountability non-negotiable, so that coordination itself can become shared infrastructure for a human-AI society that is open, pluralistic, and governable.

</details>


### [124] [Ontological Knowledge Blocks: Executable Compliance and Profile-Based Validation for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23297)
*Aasish Kumar Sharma,Julian M. Kunkel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为本体知识块（OKBs）的可编程治理基础设施，将监管义务编译为对结构化证据图的机器可检查约束，以实现AI服务的自动化合规验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于文档的合规方法难以扩展到自动化AI系统，缺乏可扩展性、动态性和机器可读性，无法满足关键数字基础设施中AI治理的需求。

Method: 设计并形式化了一个五元组结构的OKB，结合RDF/OWL概念模型、SHACL验证规则、明确的证据要求和PROV-O溯源链接，并通过确定性监管编译器将结构化的中间表示（IR）转换为可组合的知识模块。

Result: 在HPC资源分配场景中进行了24次验证运行和四种治理配置文件测试，结果显示配置文件敏感的验证行为、违规累积严格递增、SHACL验证延迟在12.6ms至100.3ms之间，并确认Combined配置文件为最全面的配置。

Conclusion: OKBs提供了一种可扩展、可验证且可重构的AI治理框架，支持机器自动审计与多配置文件合规，推动AI治理从静态文档向动态、程序化基础设施转变。

Abstract: AI-enabled services deployed in critical digital infrastructure are subject to governance obligations spanning transparency, accountability, fairness, and traceability. Compliance today remains documentation-centric: obligations are described in prose, audits rely on static checklists, and verification depends on manual review. Such approaches do not scale to automated AI systems. This paper introduces Ontological Knowledge Blocks (OKBs), a programmable governance infrastructure that compiles regulatory obligations into machine-checkable constraints over structured evidence graphs. We formalize an OKB as a 5-tuple that binds normative obligations to an RDF/OWL concept schema, executable SHACL validation rules, explicit evidence requirements, and PROV-O provenance links. A deterministic regulatory compiler translates structured Intermediate Representation (IR) records into composable KB modules, enabling profile-based governance reconfiguration without modifying service code. We implement two prototypes and evaluate them in an AI-assisted HPC resource allocation scenario across 24 validation runs and four governance profiles. Results demonstrate profile-sensitive validation, strictly additive violation accumulation, SHACL validation latency between 12.6 ms and 100.3 ms, and profile equivalence testing confirming Combined as the strictly most comprehensive profile. All artefacts are released as open source.

</details>


### [125] [Human-in-the-Loop Multi-Agent Ventilator Decision Support with Contextual Bandit Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23320)
*Sijia Li,Xiaoyu Tan,Qixing Wang,Weiyi Zhao,Chen Zhan,Teqi Hao,Xuemin Wang,Lei Gu,Roland Eils,Xihe Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于呼吸机决策支持的多智能体系统VDSS，通过人机协作和模块化设计实现个性化、可追溯且安全的临床决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的呼吸机决策支持方法如基于规则的系统难以个性化，而端到端强化学习或大语言模型系统难以控制和审计，缺乏对临床实际需求的适配。

Method: 提出VDSS框架，采用人在回路的多智能体架构，通过契约驱动的结构化接口协调模块化决策组件；使用上下文老虎机进行在线偏好适应，根据医生每次接受的决策更新个性化偏好，并利用结构化拒绝反馈触发目标重规划。

Result: 在回顾性ICU轨迹重放与专家评审中，VDSS显示出更高的推荐接受度和更少的交互轮次即可达成可接受方案。

Conclusion: VDSS通过可追溯、可审计的设计支持安全可控的个性化呼吸机决策，提升了人机协作的效率与临床可用性。

Abstract: Ventilator decision support requires sequential decisions that track evolving physiology and disease trajectories while respecting safety boundaries and clinician specific tuning styles. Rule based approaches rarely generalize personalization, and end to end reinforcement learning or single large language model systems remain difficult to control and audit. We propose the Ventilator Decision Support System (VDSS), a human in the loop multi agent framework that coordinates modular decision components through contract driven structured interfaces and produces traceable evidence for review. VDSS performs online preference adaptation with a contextual bandit, updating clinician specific preferences from the final accepted decision at each adjustment cycle and using them to guide subsequent recommendations. Structured rejection feedback triggers targeted replanning to reduce unproductive iterations and improve interaction stability. Retrospective ICU trajectory replay with expert review indicates higher recommendation acceptability and fewer interaction rounds to reach an acceptable plan, supporting clinically deployable human AI collaboration.

</details>


### [126] [EDGE-OPD: Internalizing Privileged Context with Evidence Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23493)
*Aristotelis Lazaridis,Dylan Bates,Aman Sharma,Brian King,Vincent Lu,Jack FitzGerald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的On-Policy Self-Distillation方法——EDGE-OPD，通过引导 rollout 和证据掩码机制，有效解决了特权信息引入导致的模型行为偏移问题，实现了稀有行为的学习与通用能力的保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) 虽然高效且节省资源，但因训练时使用的特权信息（如身份、私有事实）可能引发非预期的行为变化，导致模型在推理时表现下降。本文旨在解决OPS D中由特权信息带来的副作用问题，确保目标行为的有效迁移而不损害通用能力。

Method: 提出 EDGE-OPD 方法，包含两个关键机制：1）使用引导 rollout，在采样阶段将特权信息相关的行为注入学生模型，确保目标行为出现在on-policy数据中；2）应用证据掩码，仅在特权信息支持所采样token的位置更新学生模型，而非整个序列都进行更新。

Result: 实验表明，标准OPSD及其变体（包括使用验证器的RLSD）在学习目标身份任务上完全失败，而引入引导 rollout 后的方法能够成功学习；消融实验显示，persona信号主要集中在正向证据尾部区域，验证了掩码策略的有效性。

Conclusion: EDGE-OPD 通过结构化地利用特权信息，实现了对稀有目标行为（如特定身份）的有效学习，同时避免了对学生通用能力的干扰，为高效知识迁移提供了新思路。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has gained wide attraction as an LLM post-training paradigm due to its effectiveness in improving capabilities without introducing model distribution drift, and consequently, regression in general tasks. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) is an efficient use-case of OPD, which is appealing as it requires only a single model as a student and teacher, and it also has the benefit of providing privileged context that is a absent at inference time (e.g. a persona, a private fact, or a worked solution) to the teacher during the training process. The challenge in this approach is that the privileged information can change model behavior more than intended: it can modify reasoning, degrade general capabilities, and affect performance indicators like response length, style, or local token preferences. Consequently, OPSD may train the student on side effects rather than a desired, transferable behavior. In this paper, we study this problem in a rare-token/identity setting and propose EviDence GuidEd On-Policy Distillation (EDGE-OPD), a modification of OPSD with two distinct characteristics: a) it uses guided rollouts to inject privileged-context behavior to the student at sampling time, so that the rare target behavior is actually present in the on-policy data, and b) it applies an evidence mask: the student is updated only at token positions where the privileged context supports the sampled token, rather than on every token in the rollout. We empirically show that OPSD (and its variant RLSD, with and without a verifier) completely fail to learn a target identity, while the integration of guided rollouts allows them to succeed. Additionally, mask-region ablations show that the persona signal is localized to the positive-evidence tail, allows us to draw valuable insights about efficient knowledge transfer and preservation of general purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [127] [CP or DP? Why Not Both: A Case Study in the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23569)
*Emma Legrand,Roger Kameugne,Pierre Schaus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将动态规划（DP）与约束规划（CP）有效结合的方法，以解决带任意优先约束的调度问题（PSSP），其中DP作为主搜索框架，CP用于子过程的全局约束传播，展示了该混合方法的可行性与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在展示动态规划与约束规划可以有效且优雅地结合，弥补两者单独使用时的局限，尤其在处理复杂调度问题时发挥各自优势。

Method: 采用动态规划作为主框架，利用约束规划作为子程序进行全局约束传播，并结合 anytime 列搜索等灵活策略，支持任意前序图和大规模邻域搜索（LNS）设计。

Result: 所提方法能够自然处理任意优先约束图，并支持重启间重用DP模型以改进解，虽然在性能上未超越最先进的纯CP求解器，但验证了混合框架的可行性。

Conclusion: 动态规划与约束规划可以成功融合，DP提供搜索结构，CP增强剪枝能力，该集成方式为组合优化问题提供了新的建模与求解思路。

Abstract: Dynamic Programming (DP) and Constraint Programming (CP) are well-established paradigms for solving combinatorial optimization problems. Usually, these two approaches are used separately. This paper aims to show that the two can be combined effectively and elegantly, with DP serving as the primary search framework and CP used as a subroutine to leverage global constraint propagation. This paper presents such an approach for the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem (PSSP), for which a pure DP method has previously been proposed, and efficient CP filtering algorithms are available. The PSSP is a general scheduling problem where each job consists of a set of operations with arbitrary precedence constraints. The approach is flexible enough to accommodate anytime DP strategies, such as anytime column search, whereas the original DP algorithm operated in a strictly layer-wise manner. Moreover, the flexibility of the CP modeling makes it straightforward to incorporate arbitrary precedence constraints. As a result, the model naturally handles any precedence graph and even enables the design of a Large Neighborhood Search (LNS) scheme, in which the DP model is reused, and partial-order schedules are imposed across restarts to improve the incumbent solution. While not competitive with state-of-the-art pure CP solvers for this specific problem, our primary contribution is demonstrating the viability of this hybrid integration.

</details>


### [128] [Co-ReAct: Rubrics as Step-Level Collaborators for ReAct Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23590)
*Jiazheng Kang,Bowen Zhang,Zixin Song,Jiangwang Chen,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Co-ReAct，一种基于评分标准（rubric）引导的推理-行动框架，通过在推理过程中引入细粒度、步骤级的外部指导来提升搜索密集型复杂任务中智能体的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReAct类智能体依赖内部判断决定下一步操作，容易产生浅层、冗余或目标不明确的执行路径；而已有评分标准多用于事后评估或训练奖励，缺乏对决策过程的实时引导。

Method: 提出Co-ReAct框架，在每一步决策时将生成的评分标准注入智能体上下文以指导其选择‘推理’或‘行动’；训练一个专用的评分标准生成器，采用GRPO算法并优化基于多位专家排名共识的Spearman等级相关性奖励，使生成的评分标准更具区分性。

Result: 在DeepResearchBench和SQA-CS-V2两个基准上，Co-ReAct在多种开源（8B/14B）和闭源前沿模型构建的搜索智能体中均优于ReAct及其他测试时计算基线；训练好的评分标准生成器可作为即插即用组件提升现有方法性能。

Conclusion: Co-ReAct通过将高质量、步骤级的评分标准融入推理过程，有效提升了智能体在复杂多步任务中的决策质量，且具备良好的通用性和兼容性。

Abstract: ReAct-style agents for search-intensive, multi-step reasoning tasks rely largely on their own internal judgment to decide what evidence to seek, which reasoning or action step to take next, and when to stop, often producing shallow, redundant, or poorly targeted trajectories. Prior work has explored rubrics as external quality signals, but existing uses are mostly evaluative rather than action-guiding: rubrics typically serve as training-time rewards or post-hoc evaluators of completed outputs, and in deep-research settings they are often coarse-grained and report-level rather than step-level. We introduce Co-ReAct, a rubric-guided action-selection framework that uses rubrics as step-level guidance during inference. At each decision step, Co-ReAct injects a rubric into the agent's context to guide the next Reason-or-Act decision, specifying what the agent should target in evidence seeking, search, reasoning, or self-evaluation. To make this guidance reliable, we train a dedicated rubric generator with GRPO. Unlike prior pairwise or binary preference formulations, our objective optimizes a list-wise Spearman rank-correlation reward against multi-judge expert consensus rankings, encouraging rubrics that are discriminative rather than merely plausible. On DeepResearchBench and SQA-CS-V2, Co-ReAct consistently improves over ReAct and representative test-time compute baselines across search agents built on both 8B/14B open-source and frontier closed-source base models. The trained rubric generator can also serve as a drop-in component that improves these baselines without changing their underlying decision mechanisms. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZBWpro/Co-ReAct.

</details>


### [129] [Solving the Aircraft Disassembly Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23592)
*Charles Thomas,Pierre Schaus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了飞机报废拆解过程中的调度问题，提出了一种约束规划模型和一种混合整数规划模型，并基于工业合作伙伴提供的实际数据进行了测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于报废飞机拆解过程复杂且利润微薄，需要通过高效的调度来提高盈利性并促进该实践的实施。

Method: 提出了两种解决方法：约束编程（Constraint Programming）模型和混合整数规划（MIP）模型。

Result: 在包含多达1450项任务的不同规模实例上对模型进行了测试，验证了其可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型能够有效应对大规模飞机拆解调度问题，有助于提升拆解过程的效率与经济性。

Abstract: Dismantling aircrafts reaching their end of life is a complex endeavour that is necessary in terms of sustainability but yields small income margins for air transport companies. An efficient scheduling of the disassembly procedure is thus crucial to ensure the profitability of the process and incentivize practice. This is a large scheduling problem that involves thousands of tasks and many different constraints: Extracting parts that are destined to be reused requires technicians with specific certifications and equipment. Extraction operations might be subject to precedence relations. Furthermore, the aircraft must be kept balanced during the whole process. Finally, some of the locations of the aircraft have a limited space that caps the number of technicians able to work there concurrently. This article presents the problem in details and proposes two approaches to solve the problem: a Constraint Programming model and a MIP model. The models are tested on instances of varying sizes involving up to 1450 tasks, which are based on real operational data provided by an industrial partner.

</details>


### [130] [Agentic Proving for Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23772)
*Alessandro Sosso,Akhil Arora,Bas Spitters*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Claude Code在基于Lean 4的CLEVER基准上展现出卓越的程序生成与验证能力，覆盖98.8%的规范生成、87.5%的实现验证和98.1%的端到端成功率，并揭示现有评估方法的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估现代智能体定理证明系统（如Claude Code）在程序验证任务上的能力边界，尤其是在形式化代码生成与验证中的表现。

Method: 将Claude Code置于智能体证明框架中，在CLEVER（Lean 4程序验证基准）上进行评估，采用自洽前提下的端到端验证流程，并结合人工审查其自我反馈质量。

Result: Claude Code能为98.8%的问题生成有效规范（其中81.3%通过CLEVER的同构评分），对正确规范实现验证的成功率达87.5%，端到端成功率为98.1%；同时能自主识别失败原因及数据集中的残留错误。

Conclusion: 当前基于编译器闭环的智能体范式是基础性程序验证最有效的手段，但现有基准难度已落后于模型能力，亟需更严格、抗错误的评估方法，尤其是替代同构性评分的新机制。

Abstract: Agentic systems have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for automated theorem proving in formal mathematics. To assess how far these capabilities extend to program verification, we evaluate Claude Code in an agentic proving framework on CLEVER, a Lean 4 benchmark for verifiable code generation. Our results show that Claude generates arguably valid specifications for 98.8% of problems (with 81.3% also accepted by CLEVER's isomorphism-based scoring on the correct portion of the benchmark), certifies implementations against correct ground-truth specifications for 87.5% of problems, and reaches a 98.1% success rate on the end-to-end program generation and verification pipeline over entries with self-consistent premises. Across all stages, Claude further provides high-quality feedback on its own attempts (as confirmed under manual review), identifying underlying causes of failure and lingering bugs in the dataset. These findings highlight a growing mismatch between the difficulty of existing program verification benchmarks and the capabilities of modern agentic provers, and point to the need for more rigorous, bug-resilient evaluation methodologies, and in particular for alternatives to isomorphism-based scoring of generated specifications. More broadly, our results provide empirical evidence that tight compiler-in-the-loop agentic paradigms are currently the most effective approach for foundational program verification.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Binary Edits Robust Multimodal Knowledge Editing with Adversarial Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23780)
*Haoyuan Wang,Xiaohao Liu,Jiajie Su,Jianmao Xiao,Chaochao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多模态大语言模型的鲁棒内在知识编辑方法，通过引入潜在对抗增强（LAR）和秩约束子空间学习（RCSL）来提升编辑的泛化性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态知识编辑方法在语义等价的视觉和语言变体间传播编辑效果时存在泛化能力不足的问题，主要由于缺乏显式的语义监督、编辑范围僵化以及对高维空间中单个样本的偏倚锚定。

Method: 作者形式化了基于知识单元的鲁棒性定义，并提出LAR方法在联合潜在空间中生成语义一致但具有对抗性的变体以暴露脆弱语义区域；同时设计RCSL方法，在编辑层通过对奇异值的目标函数实现对抗表示的低秩对齐。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法ASAM在多个评估指标上显著提升了编辑的泛化能力和模型鲁棒性，有效解决了现有方法在多模态语义一致性与编辑传播方面的局限。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模语义等价性和低秩结构约束，可以实现更鲁棒且具泛化的多模态知识编辑，为未来多模态模型更新提供了新方向。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) need efficient mechanisms to update knowledge without degrading existing capabilities. While intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing achieves strong reliability and locality, it often exhibits limited generality, failing to propagate edits across semantically equivalent visual and linguistic variations. This issue arises from the lack of explicit semantic supervision, rigid editing scopes, and biased anchoring to individual samples in high-dimensional multimodal spaces. We address robust intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing by explicitly targeting generalization. We formalize robustness through knowledge units that group semantically equivalent multimodal inputs and define generality as consistent predictions within each unit. To expose fragile semantic regions, we introduce Latent Adversarial Robustification (LAR), which generates adversarial yet semantically coherent variants in the joint latent space. We further propose Rank-Constrained Subspace Learning (RCSL), enforcing low-rank alignment of adversarial representations at the edit layer via a singular value-based objective. Extensive analysis demonstrates the effectiveness of ASAM empirically.

</details>


### [132] [SPACENUM: Revisiting Spatial Numerical Understanding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23898)
*Jianshu Zhang,Yijiang Li,Huifeixin Chen,Haoran Lu,Letian Xue,Bingyang Wang,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为SpaceNum的统一框架，用于评估视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在空间探索和空间推理中对数字输出的真正理解能力。研究发现，当前的VLMs在将数字与空间意义结合方面表现不佳，主要依赖浅层的空间线索，难以建立稳定的坐标感知表示，并且无法从视觉观察中抽象出结构化的空间布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉-语言模型（VLMs）越来越多地部署在具身环境中并产生数值输出，但这些数值是否真正基于空间感知仍不清楚。因此，本研究旨在重新审视VLMs在空间环境中的数值理解能力。

Method: 提出了一种统一框架SpaceNum，包含两种互补设置：动态过渡中的数字和静态布局中的数字。设计了两个双向任务Num2Space和Space2Num，以评估VLMs如何在视觉端的空间结构和语言端的数值表示之间进行映射。通过错误分析、推理轨迹分析和受控干预进行系统研究。

Result: 研究发现，现有的VLMs在动态过渡和静态布局中都未能将数字真正扎根于空间意义，表现接近随机猜测。模型严重依赖浅层空间线索，难以构建稳定的坐标感知表示，并且无法从视觉观察中抽象出结构化的空间布局。显式推理仅带来微小改进，而微调可以部分提升空间数值理解和向外部空间推理基准的迁移。

Conclusion: 当前的视觉-语言模型在空间数值理解方面存在显著缺陷，需要进一步的研究和改进来实现真正的空间感知和数值接地。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in embodied environments, where they need produce numerical outputs such as action magnitudes and spatial coordinates. Although these numbers appear meaningful, it remains unclear whether these numerical outputs are genuinely grounded in spatial perception. Therefore, in this work, we revisit spatial numerical understanding through SpaceNum, a unified framework that captures two complementary settings: numbers as dynamic transitions during spatial exploration, and numbers as static layouts in spatial reasoning. We formulate two bidirectional tasks, Num2Space and Space2Num, to evaluate how well VLMs map between vision-side spatial structure and language-side numerical representations. We systematically study whether current VLMs truly understand numerical values in spatial settings. Across dynamic transitions and static layouts, we find that models largely fail to ground numbers in spatial meaning and often perform close to random guess. Through error analysis, reasoning trace analysis, and controlled interventions, we show that current VLMs rely heavily on shallow spatial cues, struggle to build stable coordinate-aware representations, and fail to abstract structured spatial layouts from visual observations. We further show that explicit reasoning provides only marginal gains, while tuning can partially improve spatial numerical understanding and transfer to external spatial reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Extending Deep Event Visual Odometry with Sparse Point-Cloud Export](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22890)
*Alireza Safdari,Sajad Ashraf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本项目在DEVO基础上扩展了稀疏点云导出流程，保留原有视觉里程计的同时实现几何场景输出，实验表明其局部一致性高但存在密度与累积误差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于事件相机在高速运动和复杂光照下的优势，希望利用DEVO已估计的3D结构生成可用于可视化和后续处理的显式稀疏点云。

Method: 提出一种不修改核心里程计的点云导出方法，提取DEVO内部3D结构并转换为显式点云表示，并实现数据导出、格式转换与点云清理的工作流。

Result: 在BOARD SLOW序列上验证，导出的稀疏点云与EMVS重建结果局部一致，在5厘米阈值下精度高，但存在密度低、完整性差和受累积里程计噪声影响的问题。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了DEVO内部结构的外部导出，支持稀疏几何输出，同时揭示了当前方案在完整性和误差累积方面的局限性。

Abstract: Event cameras are well suited for visual odometry under high-speed motion and challenging lighting conditions due to their low latency, high temporal resolution, and high dynamic range. Deep Event Visual Odometry (DEVO) demonstrated that monocular event-only odometry can achieve strong performance by combining sparse patch tracking, learned patch selection, recurrent correspondence refinement, and differentiable bundle adjustment. In this project, we extend DEVO with a sparse point-cloud export pipeline. Rather than modifying the core odometry formulation, our approach exposes the internal 3D structure already estimated by DEVO and converts it into an explicit point-cloud representation for visualization and further processing. In addition, we implement a practical workflow for data export, format conversion, and point-cloud cleanup. The resulting system preserves the original visual odometry pipeline while enabling sparse geometric scene output. Experiments on the BOARD SLOW sequence show that the exported sparse cloud is locally consistent with EMVS reconstructions, achieving high precision at a 5 cm threshold, while also highlighting the expected limitations in density, completeness, and sensitivity to accumulated odometry noise.

</details>


### [134] [Agentic-VLA: Efficient Online Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22896)
*Ruofan Jin,Zaixi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Agentic-VLA的新型视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型训练框架，通过自适应奖励合成、语言引导探索和经验记忆三项创新，显著提升了机器人操作中VLA模型在新环境中的泛化能力和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型训练方法在面对新环境时泛化能力差，且需要大量演示数据，训练效率低，难以实现持续在线适应。因此，亟需一种能够高效进行在线适应的VLA训练框架。

Method: 提出Agentic-VLA框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 自适应奖励合成，根据任务复杂度和模型当前能力动态生成奖励并分解子目标；(2) 语言引导探索，利用批评模型提供结构化探索指导；(3) 经验记忆，存储并检索策略权重以实现相似任务的快速启动。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，长视野任务性能提升+12.3%，1次学习提升+28.5%，跨任务迁移能力从0%提升至31.2%，收敛速度提高2.4倍；在双臂RoboTwin 2.0基准（包括Hard设置）上也保持优势。

Conclusion: Agentic-VLA显著推动了VLA系统向真正自适应和持续学习方向的发展，为部署中的机器人提供了高效的在线适应解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by leveraging pre-trained vision-language representations. However, current VLA training methods suffer from two critical limitations: poor generalization to novel environments and low training efficiency requiring extensive demonstrations. We introduce Agentic-VLA, an agentic training framework that enables VLAs to efficiently adapt online through three key innovations: (1) Adaptive Reward Synthesis, which dynamically generates and adjusts reward functions based on the VLA's current capabilities and task complexity, decomposing complex tasks into learnable sub-goals for curriculum learning; (2) Language-Guided Exploration, where a critic model provides structured guidance for systematic exploration rather than random sampling; and (3) Experience Memory,which stores and retrieves task-relevant policy weights for warm-starting adaptation to similar tasks. We evaluate Agentic-VLA on the LIBERO benchmark, achieving substantial improvements: +12.3% on long-horizon tasks, +28.5% in 1-shot learning, and enabling cross-task transfer from 0% to 31.2% without task-specific demonstrations. Our framework also demonstrates 2.4x faster convergence compared to existing online adaptation methods. Beyond LIBERO, Agentic-VLA retains its advantage on the dual-arm RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark, including under its randomized Hard setting. These results establish Agentic-VLA as a significant step toward truly adaptive VLA systems capable of continuous learning in deployment.

</details>


### [135] [Robots That Know What to Ask: Recovering Misaligned Rewards through Targeted Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22986)
*Helena Merker,Nick Walker,Andreea Bobu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过检测演示中未充分指定的特征并主动请求针对性纠正演示来学习奖励函数的框架，利用特征在演示中变化程度的统计信号识别不确定性，并通过自然语言解释向用户查询补充信息，显著提升了奖励函数的学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于实际演示常因人类认知负荷或训练覆盖不足而存在缺陷，导致某些任务相关特征被低估或遗漏，从而使学习到的奖励函数产生歧义并在部署时出现行为偏差。因此需要一种方法识别这些未充分指定的特征并加以修正。

Method: 提出一个基于统计信号的框架：在演示中被一致优化的特征表现稳定、变异小，而未被充分指定的特征则变异大；利用该差异识别潜在不确定特征，机器人用自然语言解释其不确定的内容，并主动请求针对这些特征的补充演示。

Result: 在模拟桌面操作环境和真实Franka机器人用户研究中验证了方法有效性；相比随机查询和被动数据收集，该方法显著提高了奖励函数恢复的准确性，有效减少了由不完美演示带来的学习歧义。

Conclusion: 通过识别演示中特征变异程度并结合自然语言解释进行主动查询，能够有效缓解因演示不完整导致的奖励函数学习歧义问题，提升人机协作中学习效率与对齐性。

Abstract: Learning reward functions from demonstrations assumes that demonstrations provide adequate supervision over all features -- or task-relevant aspects of behavior. In practice, demonstrations are often imperfect: humans may under-emphasize certain features due to cognitive load or physical difficulty, or the training regime may fail to sufficiently cover all relevant situations. In either case, important features may be underspecified, leading to ambiguity in the learned reward function and misaligned behavior at deployment. We propose a framework that detects such underspecified features and actively solicits targeted corrective demonstrations. Our key insight is that demonstrations implicitly reveal which features are well specified: features that are consistently optimized show little variation across demonstrations, while features that are underspecified vary widely. We leverage this statistical signal to infer which features may have been insufficiently demonstrated. The robot then explains which features it is uncertain about in natural language and queries for demonstrations that explicitly address the identified gaps. We evaluate our approach in a simulated tabletop manipulation domain and in a user study with a real Franka robot. Targeted, explanation-guided queries significantly improve reward recovery compared to random querying and passive data collection, reducing ambiguity that would otherwise persist in learning from imperfect demonstrations.

</details>


### [136] [Verified Task-Space Motion Planning Under Joint-Space Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22991)
*Hanjiang Hu,Changliu Liu,Yebin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于SOS验证的自适应任务空间规划方法，通过在每一步计算满足关节位移约束的可验证最大笛卡尔步长，有效避免了关节越限和目标无法到达的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的反应式任务空间规划器（如Bug2）使用固定的笛卡尔步长，忽略机械臂的关节角度限制，在雅可比矩阵条件较差时容易导致关节超出允许范围，引发轨迹漂移甚至无法到达目标。

Method: 利用逆运动学的二阶多项式近似和S-过程，构建一个小型半定规划问题，求解在关节位移约束下可验证的最大笛卡尔超矩形的半宽λ*；同时设计了一个利用二次结构的二分法，可在亚毫秒级时间内完成验证。将该证书集成到Bug2中，实现步长自适应调节。

Result: 在94个对抗性场景、六种关节限幅设置下的统计实验表明，所提方法实现了0%的关节越限率和100%的目标到达率，而标准Bug2在6%-11%的步长中出现越限，在最多18%的场景中未能到达目标。

Conclusion: 通过引入可验证的安全步长机制，显著提升了反应式任务空间规划在关节限制下的鲁棒性和可行性，解决了传统方法因固定步长导致的越限与目标不可达问题。

Abstract: Reactive task-space planners such as Bug2 operate with fixed Cartesian step sizes and are unaware of the manipulator's joint-angle limits. When the Jacobian is poorly conditioned, even small Cartesian steps can demand joint changes that exceed admissible bounds; clipping the joints to their limits causes tracking drift and can prevent goal reaching entirely. We address this by computing, at each planning step, the largest Cartesian hyperrectangle that is \emph{certifiably reachable} under joint displacement bounds. Using a second-order polynomial approximation of the inverse kinematics and the S-procedure, we formulate a small semidefinite program whose solution yields the certified half-width~$λ^\star$. An equivalent bisection procedure exploiting the quadratic structure solves the certification in sub-millisecond time. Integrating this certificate with Bug2 yields a planner whose step size adapts to local kinematic conditioning. In a statistical evaluation over 94 adversarial scenarios spanning six joint-limit settings, the SOS-verified planner achieves \emph{zero} joint-limit violations with a 100\% goal-reaching rate, whereas a standard Bug2 planner violates joint limits in 6--11\% of steps and fails to reach the goal in up to 18\% of scenarios.

</details>


### [137] [UfM*: Uncertainty from Motion* for DNN Depth Estimation Using Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23098)
*Soumya Sudhakar,Sertac Karaman,Vivienne Sze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为UfM*的高效不确定性估计方法，通过高斯混合模型比较多视角视图间的差异，仅需单次DNN推理即可实现高精度、低能耗的不确定性估计，适用于资源受限的机器人系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性估计方法（如集成和基于采样的方法）需要多次推理，计算和内存开销大，且单图像预测无法衡量同一区域在不同视角下的预测分歧。

Method: 提出UfM*算法，利用紧凑的高斯混合模型比较前后视角，通过单次DNN推理高效计算多视角分歧，并结合aleatoric不确定性提升整体性能。

Result: 在100个ScanNet序列上，相比集成方法，UfM*将预期校准误差降低24-28%，能耗仅为3%，内存占用为0.02%；在微型机器人上以30FPS实现实时运行，每张224x224图像仅消耗63mJ。

Conclusion: 使用高斯模型衡量多视角分歧可显著提升不确定性估计的效率与精度，使资源受限的机器人系统能够实时部署可靠的深度估计。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimation is critical for deploying monocular depth deep neural networks (DNNs) in safety-critical robotic systems. Conventional uncertainty methods such as ensembles and sampling-based approaches require multiple inferences per image, incurring substantial compute and memory overhead. Moreover, uncertainty predicted from a single image misses out on measuring disagreement between predictions across views of the same region. We propose Uncertainty from Motion* (UfM*), an uncertainty estimation algorithm that measures multiview disagreement efficiently by comparing previous and current views using a compact Gaussian mixture, requiring only a single DNN inference per image. Using Gaussians to compute multiview disagreement is not only more compute- and memory-efficient than a prior approach using a point cloud, but also improves uncertainty by measuring disagreement across regions of 3D space. UfM* paired with aleatoric uncertainty improves expected calibration error by 24-28% compared to an ensemble, while requiring only 3% of the energy and 0.02% of the memory on 100 out-of-distribution ScanNet sequences. We demonstrate UfM* consumes only 63 mJ per 224x224 image while running real-time at 30 FPS on an Arm Cortex-A76 CPU onboard a miniature energy-constrained robot, highlighting that measuring multiview disagreement using Gaussians enables efficient uncertainty for resource-constrained robotic systems.

</details>


### [138] [Four Simple Proprioceptive Estimators for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23100)
*Frank Dellaert,Chiyun Noh,Varun Agrawal,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一系列利用足部与环境接触来减少惯性测量单元（IMU）漂移的腿部机器人状态估计器，并提供了四种变体的开源实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于消费级IMU噪声较大导致惯性解算漂移，需要通过足部间歇性接触环境的信息来改善腿部机器人的状态估计精度。

Method: 从Hartley等人提出的接触辅助不变EKF出发，降低接触更新频率，随后用小型因子图替代测量更新，并进一步将其转化为带有接触阶段落脚点的固定滞后平滑器，考虑或不考虑演变的IMU偏差。

Result: 开发了逐步增强表达能力的状态估计器序列，在GTSAM中提供了所有四种变体的实现，并额外提供ROS2兼容版本以促进可重复性和进一步研究。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效利用足部接触信息缓解了IMU漂移问题，为纯本体感知的腿部里程计研究提供了实用且可扩展的工具。

Abstract: Legged robots carry an IMU, but the inertial solution drifts because consumer-grade IMUs are noisy. However, the feet create intermittent contacts with the environment that can be used to mitigate that drift. This report develops a sequence of increasingly expressive legged robot state estimators that leverage this. In all cases, the floating-base state comprises attitude, position, velocity, and IMU biases. To model foot contacts, we start from the contact-aided invariant EKF of Hartley et al., albeit at a reduced contact update rate. This is then augmented by replacing the measurement update by a small factor graph. Finally, we turn the same factors into a fixed-lag smoother with contact-episode footholds, with and without an evolving IMU bias. To facilitate reproducibility and further research in proprioceptive legged odometry, all four variants are available in GTSAM (Dellaert et. al), and we additionally provide a ROS2-compatible implementation.

</details>


### [139] [$π_0$-EqM: Equilibrium Matching for Closed-Loop Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23128)
*Huanming Liu,Congsheng Xu,Jianmin Ji,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了$π_0$-EqM，一种基于平衡匹配（EqM）解码器的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，用于改进机器人操作中的生成式流程匹配动作解码。相比固定采样步数的方法，该方法在推理深度上更具灵活性，并在多个任务中显著提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型通常使用固定的采样 horizon，限制了控制循环中的状态依赖计算和时间上的重用能力。为了克服这一局限性，本文旨在引入更灵活的推理机制以提升性能。

Method: 将$π_0$中的流匹配专家替换为均衡匹配（Equilibrium Matching, EqM）解码器，保持上游VLA架构不变，允许根据状态动态调整推理深度，并探索残差与任务成功之间的关系。

Result: $π_0$-EqM在300步预算下将RoboTwin的平均成功率从40.4%提升至50.2%，在LIBERO-10上达到87.0%的成功率；发现残差与成功率之间存在任务依赖的非单调关系，称为“稳定性-可执行性差距”。

Conclusion: 迭代VLA控制中的推理深度应被视为策略设计的一部分，本文提出的能量型VLA视角可能为跨任务和具身场景下的可组合动作生成提供新方向。

Abstract: Currently, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become the most adopted paradigm for robotic manipulation for its great potential for task generalization. While most generative flow-matching action decoders for VLA control are often deployed with fixed sampling horizons, limiting state-dependent compute and temporal reuse across control cycles. We present $π_0$-EqM, which replaces the flow-matching expert in $π_0$ with an Equilibrium Matching (EqM) decoder while leaving the upstream VLA stack unchanged. Under a matched 300-step budget, $π_0$-EqM improves RoboTwin average success from 40.4% to 50.2% across 19 tasks and remains competitive on LIBERO, with its clearest gain on LIBERO-10 (87.0%). Two threshold scans reveal a task-dependent non-monotonic relation between residual and success, which we term the stationarity--executability gap. The results suggest that inference depth in iterative VLA control is part of policy design and introduce an energy-based VLA perspective that may inform future work on composable action generation across tasks and embodiments.

</details>


### [140] [Semantic-Aware Guided Drone Exploration for Language-Conditioned 3D Indoor Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23160)
*Nitin Vegesna,Avideh Zakhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE是一种在未知3D室内环境中进行开放词汇探索的系统，结合语义与几何信息优化前沿选择，在保证覆盖率的同时提升物体发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中实现高效探索的同时，提高对特定语义对象的发现能力，克服传统方法忽略语义信息或牺牲覆盖率的问题。

Method: 基于FALCON系统，引入CLIP模型，通过对象中心嵌入存储、时间缓存、对象前沿检测和统一的语义-几何规划成本函数来整合语义线索。

Result: 在Matterport3D仿真中，SAGE在物体发现上优于FALCON和纯语义变体；相比FTU，探索速度快9.0至25.9倍，平均加速13.7倍，并具有更高的体积覆盖率；在真实无人机飞行实验中验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: SAGE能在不牺牲整体覆盖率的前提下，有效利用语义线索加速目标发现，适用于开放词汇的自主探索任务。

Abstract: We present Semantic-Aware Guided Exploration, SAGE, a system for open-vocabulary exploration in unknown 3D indoor environments that preserves coverage-oriented behavior while allowing semantic cues to reprioritize frontier selection. Building on the FALCON volumetric explorer, SAGE integrates Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) via four key components: object-centric embedding storage, a temporal cache that projects recent observations onto the free-unknown boundary, object frontiers for high-similarity detections, and a unified semantic-geometric planning cost. This cost function bounds semantic reweighting influence, ensuring frontiers are prioritized without sacrificing total coverage. In Matterport3D-based simulations, SAGE outperforms FALCON and a semantic-only ablation in object discovery across map-query pairs. Compared to Finding Things in the Unknown (FTU), SAGE completes exploration 9.0 to 25.9 times faster across the nine shared map-query pairs, achieving a mean speedup of 13.7. Furthermore, SAGE achieves substantially higher volumetric throughput than FTU. Finally, we deploy SAGE in five real-world flights in two environments on a Modal AI Starling 2 quadrotor with onboard sensing and planning, and offboard CLIP inference. Comparing SAGE and FALCON, we find that while FALCON results in faster exploration and shorter mapping trajectories, SAGE outperforms FALCON in terms of object discovery.

</details>


### [141] [Autonomous Frontier-Based Exploration with VLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23165)
*Aarush Aitha,Avideh Zakhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用视觉语言模型（VLM）进行高层决策的自主机器人探索新方法，在模拟环境中显著提升了地图覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人探索依赖几何启发式方法，缺乏上下文空间推理能力，限制了在复杂未知环境中的探索效率。

Method: 构建一个结合VLM与传统低层控制栈的探索管道，在决策点通过多模态提示（地图和潜在路径图像）让VLM选择最具前景的前沿区域。

Result: 在六个室内环境的模拟中验证，相比现有方法地图覆盖率最高提升24%。

Conclusion: 该方法轻量、无需训练且易于迁移，有效增强了机器人在未知危险环境中的探索能力。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic exploration of unknown and hazardous environments, a long-standing challenge, can be significantly improved by leveraging the advanced reasoning of Vision-Language Models (VLMs). We introduce a novel exploration pipeline where a VLM performs high-level strategic decision-making, guiding a conventional low-level robotics control stack. At decision points, the robot generates a multimodal prompt with its current map and visual imagery of potential paths, or frontiers. The VLM analyzes this prompt to select the most promising frontier, replacing simple geometric heuristics with contextual spatial reasoning. This approach, validated in simulation across six indoor environments, improves map coverage by up to 24\% over existing methods. Our pipeline is lightweight, training-free, and easily transferable to any robot with standard sensors and an internet connection.

</details>


### [142] [Signal Temporal Logic Motion Planning via Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23240)
*Yu Chen,Ancheng Hou,Mingyang Feng,Xiao Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合时间自动机与凸集图（GCS）的高效框架，用于在信号时序逻辑（STL）规范下进行连续时间运动规划，能够生成满足高阶逻辑、时序要求及动力学约束的平滑轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动规划方法难以同时处理复杂的时序逻辑任务与平滑性、动力学约束，尤其是在高维系统中。因此，需要一种既能保证任务可满足性又能生成可执行轨迹的方法。

Method: 将STL规范转化为时间自动机，并与构型空间的凸分解结合构建联合转移系统；在此基础上，将STL运动规划问题转化为凸集图上的最短路径问题，通过求解该问题生成满足条件的Bézier样条轨迹。

Result: 该方法在多个仿真平台（包括3D四旋翼、30自由度人形机器人）和UR-3机械臂硬件实验中验证了有效性，能高效解决复杂STL任务并生成平滑、可执行的轨迹；且凸松弛形式具有多项式级计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保证任务满足性和轨迹平滑性的前提下，显著提升了STL导向的连续时间运动规划效率，适用于高维系统与实际机器人应用。

Abstract: This paper investigates continuous-time motion planning under Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications. The goal is to generate smooth robot trajectories that satisfy high-level logical and timing requirements while respecting low-level motion constraints. To this end, we propose an efficient framework that combines timed-automata reasoning with graphs of convex sets (GCS). An STL specification is first represented by a timed automaton, which is then coupled with a convex decomposition of the configuration space to form a joint transition system encoding both task progress and region occupancy. Based on this joint transition system, the STL motion-planning problem is reformulated as a shortest-path problem over a GCS, whose solution induces a smooth Bézier-spline trajectory satisfying the STL specification, smoothness requirements, and velocity bounds. We establish the soundness of the proposed formulation and analyze its computational complexity, showing that, once the timed automaton and convex decomposition are fixed, the convex relaxation scales polynomially with the configuration-space dimension and the Bézier degree. We further develop a compact timed-automaton construction for an expressive STL fragment using dedicated templates and Boolean composition. Numerical experiments on low-dimensional benchmarks, a $3$-D quadrotor, a $30$-DoF humanoid, and a hardware experiment on a UR-3 robot arm demonstrate that the proposed method efficiently solves complex STL motion-planning problems and produces smooth executable trajectories.

</details>


### [143] [Turning Adaptation into Assets: Cross-Domain Bridging for Online Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23257)
*Zixuan Hu,Xuantuo Huang,Yancheng Li,Yichun Hu,Shengyong Xu,Ling-Yu Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为IDEA的新型测试时自适应框架，通过构建动态资产库和跨域桥接实现视觉-语言导航在非平稳环境下的持续自适应，有效避免了灾难性遗忘和负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN的测试时自适应方法多采用瞬态孤立更新，易导致灾难性遗忘和负迁移，难以应对真实场景中的非平稳环境变化。

Method: 引入基于Fisher权重优化的软提示来捕获可迁移知识，并结合领域坐标构建动态资产库；利用该库将目标域投影到历史知识凸包中，形成跨域桥接，构建资产积累与初始化的互补循环。

Result: 在REVERIE、R2R和R2R-CE等多个基准上实验表明，IDEA显著优于现有方法，能实现无需训练的自适应并支持资产共享。

Conclusion: IDEA为视觉-语言导航任务提供了一种有效的持续在线自适应机制，通过资产积累与跨域桥接解决了非平稳环境下的知识更新难题。

Abstract: Navigating under non-stationary environment shifts poses a critical challenge for a Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) agent deployed in the wild. Yet, existing Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) methods for VLN largely treat online adaptation as transient, isolated updates, leading to catastrophic forgetting and negative transfer. To overcome these issues, we propose Inter-Domain BridgE with Historical Assets (IDEA), a novel TTA framework that transforms adaptation into the accumulation and composition of assets. Specifically, IDEA introduces soft prompts optimized via a Fisher-guided weighting scheme to capture the transferable knowledge. These optimized prompts are then augmented with domain coordinates to form a dynamic asset library. Leveraging this library, IDEA constructs a cross-domain bridge by projecting the target domain onto the convex hull of historical knowledge. These designs form a complementary loop: the evolving library underpins bridge construction, while the bridge provides superior initialization to accelerate asset optimization. Extensive experiments across REVERIE, R2R, and R2R-CE benchmarks demonstrate the consistent superiority of IDEA over existing methods, showcasing its ability to enable training-free adaptation via asset sharing.

</details>


### [144] [Sparse Compositional Flow Matching by geometric assembly from motion primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23341)
*Yan Tang,Yuanbo Tang,Tingyu Cao,Shaolun Huang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于可重用运动基元的组合生成框架，直接在物理轨迹空间中进行组合，通过字典学习和几何约束下的稀疏流匹配实现更高效、准确的机器人轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型将具身轨迹视为逐点生成的密集信号，忽略其潜在的结构特性，导致样本效率低；而实际任务中存在可复用的运动片段，应显式建模为组合结构以支持子任务分解。

Method: 引入运动基元字典学习，每个原子带有可学习长度掩码和起始指示符，使其成为可原样复用的运动基元；结合结构化稀疏流匹配与几何约束，通过持续时间感知的分词方法生成二进制放置矩阵，并利用可微几何损失保证相邻基元间的空间连续性和时间连贯性。

Result: 在Open X-Embodiment和3DMoTraj数据集上达到最优性能，FDE/ ADE比率从1.8降至1.07，相比最强基线A DE提升19.2%，FDE提升21.0%。

Conclusion: 直接在物理轨迹空间中进行组合生成能有效建模具身轨迹的结构性与可复用性，显著提升生成精度与效率，为具身智能中的运动生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Embodied trajectories, such as the executable motion sequences of robotic manipulators, underwater vehicles, and mobile robots, are a fundamental output of embodied AI. Modern generative models often treat them as a dense, monolithic signal generated point by point, fitting an intricate high-dimensional posterior while leaving the data's latent structure unmodeled, the same sample inefficiency long identified by the structured generative model literature. We argue that a compositional latent structure is a natural choice: many embodied tasks share recurring motion fragments that can be made explicit as a finite repertoire of reusable motion primitives, and compositional units naturally align with subtask boundaries to support task decomposition. Existing compositional generators, however, compose in a latent space and rely on post-hoc decoding to relate sampled units to actual trajectory segments. We instead compose directly in the physical trajectory space through a flow-matching framework with two coupled designs. Motion-Primitive Dictionary Learning equips each atom with a learnable length mask and binary starting indicators so the atom itself is the primitive, reused verbatim wherever it is placed. Structural Sparse Flow Matching with Geometric Constraints then generates a binary placement matrix using duration-aware tokenization and a differentiable geometric loss that enforces spatial continuity and temporal contiguity where adjacent primitives meet. On Open X-Embodiment and 3DMoTraj, the framework attains state-of-the-art accuracy and reduces the FDE/ADE ratio from 1.8 to 1.07, improving ADE by 19.2% and FDE by 21.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [145] [Multi-Floor Exploration for Ground Robots via an Incremental Reachable Graph and Structural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23350)
*Zhiwen Zhu,Jiaqi Chen,Xiangyi Huang,Meiqi Hu,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于增量可达图的多楼层探索框架，通过稀疏图结构和假设图投影提升地面机器人在复杂多层环境中的探索效率和建图完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统2D/2.5D地图无法表示楼梯、斜坡等重叠可通行表面，导致地面机器人在多楼层建筑中自主探索困难。

Method: 构建增量式可达图作为稀疏图结构，保留潜在有效的连通性；利用已探索楼层的任务区域先验信息投影到目标楼层以初始化假设图，并随观测数据逐步修正；采用分层规划器联合推理已确认与假设结构进行全局引导。

Result: 在仿真中相比基线方法提升了探索效率和建图完整性，在真实世界实验中验证了该方法的实际可行性和实时性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效支持地面机器人在多楼层环境中实现稳定、物理可达的前沿检测与高效探索。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration of multi-floor buildings remains challenging for ground robots because conventional 2D and 2.5D maps cannot represent overlapping traversable surfaces such as stairs, ramps, and multiple reachable elevations. This letter presents a multi-floor exploration framework based on an incremental reachable graph. Built as a sparse graph over reachable support surfaces, the graph preserves potentially valid connectivity through tentative graph elements under sparse observations and enables stable, physically reachable frontier detection. To guide exploration beyond the currently mapped floor, we project task-zone priors from an explored floor to initialize a hypothetical graph on the target floor and reconcile it incrementally with incoming observations. A hierarchical planner then jointly reasons over confirmed and hypothetical structures for global guidance. In simulation, the proposed method demonstrates improved exploration efficiency and mapping completeness compared to evaluated baselines. Furthermore, onboard real-world experiments validate its practical feasibility and real-time performance.

</details>


### [146] [Droneulator: A Portable UAV Simulator for Agricultural Workflows with RotorPy and Godot 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23386)
*Jacob Swindell,Michael Lowen,Marija Popovic,Riccardo Polvara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Droneulator是一个集成高保真3D场景、飞行器动力学和机器人中间件的便携式农业无人机模拟器，支持多种应用如数据采集、局部路径规划与强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业无人机研究需要一个兼具真实感、高保真动力学和通用性的模拟平台，且能部署于异构开发设备上。

Method: 结合RotorPy实现多旋翼动力学，使用Godot 4进行渲染和传感器模拟，并通过Zenoh构建ROS 2兼容的数据流管道，支持PX4控制与WebSocket轻量控制接口。

Result: 在三种农业UAV工作流中验证：基于COLMAP的三维重建图像采集、基于EGO-Planner的树冠避障局部规划、基于自定义Gymnasium环境的闭环强化学习；系统表现出低延迟感知、高质量数据采集、无碰撞规划和稳定的深度感知导航策略训练能力。

Conclusion: Droneulator提供了一个统一、可部署的模拟架构，能够支持农业无人机的检测、规划与学习任务，具备广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Agricultural UAV research requires simulators that integrate realistic 3D scenes, high-fidelity vehicle dynamics, and robotics middleware, while remaining practical to deploy across heterogeneous development machines. We present Droneulator, a portable UAV simulator architecture that combines RotorPy for multirotor dynamics with Godot 4 for rendering and sensor generation. Droneulator exposes both PX4-based control and a lightweight WebSocket command path, and publishes synchronised visual and state streams through a Zenoh-based ROS~2-compatible pipeline. This integration enables a single stack to support inspection-oriented data capture, ROS~2/PX4 local planning, and reinforcement learning experiments without modifying the simulator infrastructure. We present quantified validation of the current system across three agricultural UAV workflows: tree-scale image collection for 3D reconstruction with COLMAP, local planning around canopy obstacles using EGO-Planner, and closed-loop reinforcement learning through a custom Gymnasium environment. In the reported setup, the results show that the simulator can sustain low-latency sensing, support reconstruction-oriented data collection under varying capture density, execute collision-free local planning around canopy obstacles, and support stable depth-sensing-based policy training for obstacle-aware navigation. Together, these results show the potential of Droneulator for agricultural UAV inspection, planning, and learning within one deployable stack.

</details>


### [147] [TactileReflex: Noise-Statistics-Driven Vision-Tactile Reflex Control for Force-Sensitive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23568)
*Ziyan Feng,Yulong Fu,Zheng Li,Yuxin He,Jieji Ren,Lujia Wang,Jinni Zhou,Yudong Zhong,Qiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TactileReflex，一种基于视觉触觉感知和噪声统计标定的反射控制方法，实现对易变形容器的实时、自适应抓握力控制，无需外部标定或手动调参，在动态倒水任务中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操纵脆弱的可变形容器（如装有液体的一次性塑料杯）需要在极窄的力范围内实时调整抓握力，现有方法难以兼顾防滑与防过度变形。

Method: 通过分析触觉传感器的本征噪声特性（短时静止-释放-卸载流程），直接推导控制器阈值；利用双视觉触觉传感器提取剪切强度（$S_y$）、接触强度（$F_n$）和压心位置（$C$），构建三通道闭环反射控制器（~12 Hz），分别用于防滑、自适应释重和过力保护。

Result: 消融实验显示完整三通道系统成功防止容器永久变形（5/5成功，部分配置最多1/5）；在动态倒水任务中，固定力度基线全部失败（0/10），而TactileReflex在两种水量下达到9/10成功率。

Conclusion: TactileReflex是一种无需外部力校准、无需手动调参、不依赖材料模型的即插即用型安全控制层，可集成于高阶操作框架（如无触觉反馈的VR遥操作或视觉-语言-动作策略）之下，提升对脆弱物体操作的鲁棒性与安全性。

Abstract: Manipulating fragile deformable containers, such as disposable plastic cups filled with liquid, demands real-time grip-force adaptation within an extremely narrow force margin: insufficient force causes slip, while excessive force irreversibly deforms the thin wall. Existing approaches struggle to achieve such force-sensitive manipulation tasks. We propose a noise-statistics-based calibration-driven reflex control paradigm with vision-based tactile sensing: by analyzing the sensor's intrinsic noise characteristics (via a brief static-hold-and-unload protocol), we directly derive all controller thresholds, eliminating external force calibration, trial-and-error manual tuning, or material-specific physical models. Instantiating this paradigm, we present TactileReflex, a three-channel closed-loop controller that extracts three image-level proxies, shear intensity ($S_y$), contact intensity ($F_n$), and center of pressure ($C$), from dual visuo-tactile sensors and drives prioritized reflex channels at ~12 Hz for slip suppression, weight-adaptive release, and force protection. Each channel closes the loop directly on its proxy via noise-derived thresholds. Ablation demonstrates that only the full three-channel system is able to prevent irreversible container deformation (5/5 success vs. at most 1/5 for partial configurations). In a dynamic pouring task, fixed-effort baselines fail in all 10 attempts due to pose drift, while TactileReflex achieves 9/10 success across two water volumes. As a self-contained and interpretable controller, TactileReflex can serve as a plug-and-play safety layer beneath high-level manipulation pipelines, including haptic-free VR teleoperation and vision-language-action (VLA) policies.

</details>


### [148] [Vision-Based Agile Landing on Turbulent Waters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23717)
*Dimosthenis Angelis,Leonard Bauersfeld,Davide Scaramuzza,Evangelos Boukas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的自主多旋翼无人机在海上移动平台着陆的方法，无需显式平台状态信息，通过仿真训练并在真实环境中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于在开阔海域条件下飞行器和着陆平台的耦合运动，无人机在海上船只上的自主着陆具有挑战性，现有方法通常依赖于精确的平台状态估计，限制了实际应用。

Method: 采用强化学习方法，利用多旋翼的状态测量和从着陆表面提取的局部视觉特征（关键点及其描述符）来预测姿态和推力指令，并由传统的低层控制器执行；策略在仿真中使用合成的关键点和随机生成的归一化描述符进行训练，实现不同特征提取器的零样本部署。

Result: 在逼真的模拟器中评估表明，该方法在对应于“非常粗糙”海况下的平台运动中优于最先进的模型预测控制基线；并通过大量真实世界实验验证了使用两种不同局部特征提取器的自主机载着陆能力。

Conclusion: 这是首个不依赖显式平台状态表示即可实现湍流水域中敏捷多旋翼无人机在海上平台着陆的方法，具备良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Autonomous landing of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles on maritime vessels is challenging due to the coupled motion of the vehicle and landing platform in open-sea conditions. This paper presents a reinforcement-learning-based approach for autonomous multirotor landing on moving maritime platforms without requiring explicit platform-state information. The proposed method uses multirotor state measurements together with local visual features, consisting of keypoints and associated descriptors extracted from the landing surface, to predict attitude and thrust commands. These commands are tracked by a conventional low-level controller. The policy is trained in simulation using synthetic keypoints with randomly generated normalized descriptors, enabling zero-shot deployment with different local feature extractors onboard the UAV. We evaluate the method in a realistic simulator and show that it outperforms a state-of-the-art Model Predictive Control baseline under platform motions corresponding to ``Very Rough'' sea conditions. Finally, we perform extensive real-world experiments, demonstrating autonomous onboard landing using two different local feature extractors. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first approach for agile multirotor landing on maritime platforms in turbulent waters that does not rely on an explicit platform-state representation.

</details>


### [149] [Instrumentation for Imitation Learning: Enhancing Training Datasets for Clothes Hanger Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23847)
*Remko Proesmans,Thomas Lips,Francis wyffels*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过在物体中集成传感器（instrumentation）来提升机器人操作中的模仿学习效率，实验表明利用仪器数据的策略显著优于纯视觉方法，并能有效指导无仪器策略的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型行为模型虽推动了机器人操作的发展，但高数据需求限制了其广泛应用；本文旨在探索通过引入物体内部传感器获取状态信息，以降低学习成本并提高性能。

Method: 采用180次遥操作演示训练扩散策略，比较有无仪器数据时的策略表现，并利用仪器专家策略生成的数据增强遥操作数据集，训练纯视觉学生策略。

Result: 使用仪器数据的策略比仅用视觉的策略性能高出14-25个百分点，且展现出更强的任务意识；黑箱模仿学习策略能自主优先使用仪器信号；通过数据增强，纯视觉学生策略可达到与仪器专家相当的表现。

Conclusion: 仪器化是提升机器人操作中模仿学习效率的有效途径，为未来低数据需求、高性能的机器人学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large behaviour models have transformed the field of robotic manipulation, but prohibitive data requirements have thus far prevented a revolution similar to vision language models. We believe that instrumentation, i.e. sensor integration in objects, can provide invaluable state information and enable efficient learning for robotic manipulation. In this paper, we present instrumented imitation learning of clothes hanger insertion. Using 180 teleoperated demonstrations, we train diffusion policies with and without access to instrumentation data. Results show that policies leveraging instrumentation outperform vision-only counterparts by 14-25 %pt and exhibit greater task awareness. Crucially, a black-box imitation learning policy learns to prioritise instrumentation signals without explicit guidance. In addition, enhancing the teleoperation dataset with rollouts from an instrumented expert policy, enables a vision-only student policy to achieve performance comparable to the instrumented expert, thereby surpassing the original vision-only policy. These findings establish instrumentation as a promising strategy to enhance imitation learning for robotic manipulation. Datasets are available on Zenodo.

</details>


### [150] [Point Tracking Improves World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23856)
*Jiarui Guan,Wenshuai Zhao,Yue Pei,Ziliang Chen,Arno Solin,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JOPAT是一种联合像素与点迹的去噪扩散Transformer模型，用于机器人策略学习中的世界-动作建模，通过结合隐式视觉观测、2D点迹及动作预测，提升对长时程、遮挡和出屏运动等复杂场景的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于像素的世界-动作模型容易受光照、纹理等无关视觉变化影响，且难以处理遮挡和长时程动态，导致策略学习性能下降。

Method: 提出JOPAT模型，统一建模像素观测、2D点迹（含可见性）和动作序列，利用点迹显式表达运动信息，并采用去噪扩散Transformer进行联合预测。

Result: 在LIBERO和真实LeRobot任务上，JOPAT优于纯像素基线方法，尤其在涉及遮挡、物体交互和出屏运动的长时程任务中表现更优。

Conclusion: 结合点迹与像素的联合建模能有效解耦动态因素与视觉噪声，提升世界-动作模型的鲁棒性和实用性，尤其适用于复杂机器人操作任务。

Abstract: Robot policy learning benefits from world-action models that capture environment dynamics, but pixel-level prediction entangles dynamics with nuisance factors such as lighting and texture, making learned representations vulnerable to task-irrelevant visual variation. We propose JOPAT, a JOint Pixel-And-Track World-Action Model that predicts latent visual observations, 2D point tracks with visibility, and actions in a single denoising diffusion transformer. The key insight is that tracks provide an explicit representation of motion that captures long-horizon dynamics and remains robust under occlusion or partial out-of-frame motion, offering greater utility than modeling pixel appearance alone. On LIBERO and real-world LeRobot tasks, JOPAT improves over pixel-based baselines, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks involving occlusion, object interaction, and off-screen motion.

</details>


### [151] [Robotic Strawberry Harvesting with Robust Vision and Deep Reinforcement Learning based Sim-to-Real Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23863)
*Al Bashir,Shao-Yang Chang,Partho Ghose,Prem Raj,Chen-Kang Huang,Azlan Zahid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种闭环机器人草莓采摘系统，结合了改进的视觉模型HRAttnEdge-YOLO26-seg和基于仿真训练的深度强化学习控制（PPO），在温室中实现了84.3%的整体采摘成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统农业机器人依赖规划器进行目标到达操作，存在硬件依赖性强、开发成本高、策略训练效率低等问题，难以适应复杂多变的农业环境。因此需要一种更高效、稳定的闭环采摘方案。

Method: 设计了一个包含改进YOLO26-seg结构的实例分割模型HRAttnEdge-YOLO26-seg，引入高分辨率P2分支、分割路径注意力和边缘监督原型学习；同时在Isaac Lab中训练目标条件下的PPO策略，并通过ROS部署到UR10e机械臂上实现闭环控制。

Result: 所提视觉模型在自建和公开数据集上分割性能提升10%-14%；PPO控制器相比基于逆运动学的MoveIt基线产生更平稳动态的动作；温室试验中完成281次采摘，达到96.6%到达成功率、91.3%抓取拉取成功率和84.3%整体成功率。

Conclusion: 任务特定的感知与仿真训练的PPO控制相结合，可作为传统依赖规划器方法的有效替代，为复杂农业环境中可靠、资源高效的闭环机器人采摘提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: This study presents a closed-loop robotic strawberry harvesting system that combines a robust vision module, simulation-trained deep reinforcement learning (DRL) control, and ROS-based realrobot execution. For perception, we propose HRAttnEdge-YOLO26-seg, a modified YOLO26-seg architecture that incorporates a high-resolution P2 branch, segmentation-path attention, and edgesupervised prototype learning to improve instance segmentation in cluttered scenes. For control, we train a target-conditioned Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) policy in Isaac Lab to produce smooth joint-position commands for a UR10e manipulator and deploy it on a UR10e robot for targetfruit reaching and harvesting. This simulation-based approach reduces hardware dependency, lowers development cost, and allows scalable policy training without exhaustive physical trials before real deployment. The proposed vision model demonstrated the highest overall performance among the evaluated methods. On both self-collected and public datasets, the model showed a 10 to 14% improvement in segmentation performance. In controlled in-house tests, the PPO controller produced stable and dynamically smoother motion than a inverse kinematics (IK)-based MoveIt baseline. In greenhouse trials, the proposed integrated system harvested 281 strawberries, achieving 96.6% reaching success, 91.3% grasp-and-pull success, and 84.3% overall harvesting success. These results illustrate that task-specific perception combined with simulation-trained PPO can serve as a practical and resource-efficient alternative to conventional planner-dependent reaching in manipulation, enabling reliable closed-loop robotic harvesting in complex agricultural environments.

</details>
